@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy.reference_comparison : ∀ {m : Nat}
  {law : NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law m}
  {Result : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Type u_1} {Information : Type u_2}
  {routine : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine m law Result Information}
  {errorBound : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law → Real},
  @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.HasRiemannAccuracy m law Result Information routine errorBound →
    ∀ (problem : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem m law)
      (admitted : @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.domain m law Result Information routine problem),
      ∃ reference,
        And
          (@NumStability.IsRiemannData (Fin m → Real)
            (fun x =>
              @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.field m law problem reference x
                (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)))
            (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.left m law problem)
            (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.right m law problem))
          (And
            (@NumStability.IsOneDimensionalCellAverage (Fin m → Real)
              (@Pi.normedAddCommGroup (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i => Real.normedAddCommGroup)
              (@Pi.normedSpace Real Real.normedField (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m)
                (fun i =>
                  @NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup Real
                    (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing Real
                      (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing Real
                        (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing Real Real.normedCommRing))))
                fun i =>
                @InnerProductSpace.toNormedSpace Real Real Real.instRCLike
                  (@NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup Real
                    (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing Real
                      (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing Real
                        (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing Real Real.normedCommRing))))
                  (@RCLike.toInnerProductSpaceReal Real Real.instRCLike))
              (fun τ =>
                @NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Law.flux m law
                  (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.field m law problem reference
                    (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero)) τ))
              (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero))
              (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Problem.duration m law problem)
              (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.meanFlux m law problem reference))
            (And
              (@LE.le Real Real.instLE (@OfNat.ofNat Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0 Real Real.instZero))
                (errorBound problem))
              (And
                (@LE.le Real Real.instLE
                  (@Norm.norm (Fin m → Real)
                    (@NormedRing.toNorm (Fin m → Real)
                      (@Pi.normedRing (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                        @NormedCommRing.toNormedRing Real Real.normedCommRing))
                    (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
                      (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
                      (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.flux m law Result Information routine problem
                        admitted)
                      (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.meanFlux m law problem reference)))
                  (errorBound problem))
                (∀ (physicalMean : Fin m → Real) (comparisonBound : Real),
                  @LE.le Real Real.instLE
                      (@Norm.norm (Fin m → Real)
                        (@NormedRing.toNorm (Fin m → Real)
                          (@Pi.normedRing (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                            @NormedCommRing.toNormedRing Real Real.normedCommRing))
                        (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
                          (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
                          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Reference.meanFlux m law problem reference)
                          physicalMean))
                      comparisonBound →
                    @LE.le Real Real.instLE
                      (@Norm.norm (Fin m → Real)
                        (@NormedRing.toNorm (Fin m → Real)
                          (@Pi.normedRing (Fin m) (fun a => Real) (Fin.fintype m) fun i =>
                            @NormedCommRing.toNormedRing Real Real.normedCommRing))
                        (@HSub.hSub (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real) (Fin m → Real)
                          (@instHSub (Fin m → Real) (@Pi.instSub (Fin m) (fun a => Real) fun i => Real.instSub))
                          (@NumStability.LocalRiemannInformation.Routine.flux m law Result Information routine problem
                            admitted)
                          physicalMean))
                      (@HAdd.hAdd Real Real Real (@instHAdd Real Real.instAdd) (errorBound problem) comparisonBound)))))
